| ai_credits_exhausted_body | AI-powered features are now disabled for this organization. To re-enable them, please contact {0} to add more credits. | AI-functies zijn nu uitgeschakeld voor deze organisatie. Neem contact op met {0} om meer tegoed toe te voegen en deze functies opnieuw in te schakelen. | Body paragraph explaining that AI features are disabled and how to re-enable them. {0} is the sales contact email address. |
| ai_credits_exhausted_cta | Contact sales | Contact opnemen met verkoop | Call-to-action button text for the AI credits exhaustion email, linking to the sales contact. |
| ai_credits_exhausted_intro | Organization {0} has exhausted its AI credits. | Organisatie {0} heeft het AI-tegoed opgebruikt. | Intro paragraph for AI credits exhaustion notification. {0} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_exhausted_subject | [{0}] AI credits exhausted for "{1}" | [{0}] AI-tegoed uitgeput voor "{1}" | Subject for email sent to organization admins when AI credits are exhausted. {0} is the product name and {1} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_expired_body | These credits were unused and have been removed from the organization's balance. To purchase more credits, go to Billing. | Dit tegoed is niet gebruikt en is van het saldo van de organisatie afgeschreven. Ga naar Facturering om meer tegoed te kopen. | Body paragraph explaining the expired credits were removed and how to buy more, via the organization's Billing page. |
| ai_credits_expired_cta | Go to Billing | Naar Facturering | Call-to-action button text for the AI credits expiration email, linking to the organization's Billing page. |
| ai_credits_expired_intro | {0} AI credits of organization {1} have reached their expiration date. | {0} AI-tegoed van organisatie {1} heeft de vervaldatum bereikt. | Intro paragraph for AI credits expiration notification. {0} is the number of expired credits and {1} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_expired_subject | [{0}] AI credits expired for "{1}" | [{0}] AI-tegoed verlopen voor "{1}" | Subject for email sent to organization admins when AI credits expire. {0} is the product name and {1} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_order_status_canceled | canceled | geannuleerd | Status of an AI credits order that was canceled, inserted as {1} in "Order {0} was marked as {1}". |
| ai_credits_order_status_chargeback | charged back | teruggevorderd | Status of an AI credits order whose card payment was reclaimed by the cardholder's bank after a lost chargeback dispute, inserted as {1} in "Order {0} was marked as {1}". |
| ai_credits_order_status_refund | refunded | terugbetaald | Status of an AI credits order that was refunded, inserted as {1} in "Order {0} was marked as {1}". |
| ai_credits_order_status_reversed | reversed | teruggeboekt | Status of an AI credits order whose authorized payment was reversed, inserted as {1} in "Order {0} was marked as {1}". |
| ai_credits_refund_deducted_detail | Order {0} was marked as {1}, so {2} credits were deducted. Current balance: {3} credits. | Bestelling {0} is gemarkeerd als {1}, waardoor {2} credits zijn afgeschreven. Huidig saldo: {3} credits. | Detail paragraph explaining the deduction. {0} is the order reference, {1} is the localized refund/cancel status the order transitioned to (see ai_credits_order_status_*), {2} is the locale-formatted credits deducted, {3} is the locale-formatted current wallet balance. |
| ai_credits_refund_deducted_intro | AI credits previously granted to organization {0} have been reversed. | Eerder toegekend AI-tegoed van organisatie {0} is teruggedraaid. | Intro paragraph for the AI credits deduction notification. {0} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_refund_deducted_subject | [{0}] AI credits adjusted for "{1}" | [{0}] AI-tegoed aangepast voor "{1}" | Subject for email sent to organization admins when previously granted AI credits are deducted because the order was refunded/canceled after being processed as a purchase. {0} is the product name and {1} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_topup_skipped_detail | Order {0} had already been marked as {1} before the purchase confirmation arrived, so no credits were added. Current balance: {2} credits. | Bestelling {0} was al gemarkeerd als {1} voordat de aankoopbevestiging binnenkwam, dus er is geen tegoed toegevoegd. Huidig saldo: {2} credits. | Detail paragraph explaining why the top-up was skipped. {0} is the order reference, {1} is the localized refund/cancel status the order already had (see ai_credits_order_status_*), {2} is the locale-formatted current wallet balance. |
| ai_credits_topup_skipped_intro | An AI credits purchase for organization {0} was not applied. | Een aankoop van AI-tegoed voor organisatie {0} is niet toegepast. | Intro paragraph for the skipped AI credits top-up notification. {0} is the organization name. |
| ai_credits_topup_skipped_subject | [{0}] AI credits top-up skipped for "{1}" | [{0}] AI-tegoed oplading overgeslagen voor "{1}" | Subject for email sent to organization admins when an AI credits purchase's top-up is skipped because the order was already refunded/canceled. {0} is the product name and {1} is the organization name. |

| authentication.social.sign.up.info | Users who sign up with Google or GitHub do not receive a role automatically. An organization administrator must assign a role to grant appropriate access. {line_break}{line_break} If a social provider is later disabled, users who originally signed up with it can select "Forgot my password" to set a password and continue signing in with their email address. | Gebruikers die zich aanmelden met Google of GitHub krijgen niet automatisch een rol. Een organisatiebeheerder moet een rol toewijzen om de juiste toegang te verlenen. {line_break}{line_break} Als een sociale provider later wordt uitgeschakeld, kunnen gebruikers die zich oorspronkelijk daarmee hebben aangemeld, “Wachtwoord vergeten” selecteren om een wachtwoord in te stellen en zich vervolgens blijven aanmelden met hun e-mailadres. | Title for users without a platform role |

| project.access.refresh.hint | Once an owner adds you, refresh the page to open the project. | Zodra een eigenaar je heeft toegevoegd, ververs je de pagina om het project te openen. | Footer hint on the page shown to a user who opens a project they are not a member of. |
| project.access.request.again.at | You can request access again at {time}. | Je kunt om {time} opnieuw toegang aanvragen. | Tells when the user may request access to a project again. {time} is a time of day, e.g. 11:42 AM. |
| project.access.request.again.button | Request access again | Opnieuw toegang aanvragen | Button that asks the owners of a project for access to it once more. Disabled until the previous request is an hour old. |
| project.access.request.ask.owner | Ask a project owner to add you to this project. | Vraag een projecteigenaar om je aan dit project toe te voegen. | Shown instead of the access request form when the server cannot send emails. |
| project.access.request.description | You don't have access to this project. Only project members can open its files, branches and publications. | Je hebt geen toegang tot dit project. Alleen projectleden kunnen de bestanden, branches en publicaties ervan openen. | Description on the page shown to a user who opens a project they are not a member of. Must not name the project. |
| project.access.request.hint | Request access and the project owners will be notified by email. | Vraag toegang aan en de projecteigenaren krijgen een melding per e-mail. | Explains what requesting access to a project does. |
| project.access.request.message.label | Message to owners (optional) | Bericht aan de eigenaren (optioneel) | Label of the optional message a user sends to the owners of a project when requesting access to it. |
| project.access.request.message.placeholder | Tell the owners why you need access | Laat de eigenaren weten waarom je toegang nodig hebt | Placeholder of the optional message a user sends to the owners of a project when requesting access to it. |
| project.access.request.pending.sent.on | Request pending · sent {date} | Aanvraag in behandeling · verzonden op {date} | Status of a request for access to a project sent before today. {date} is a short date, e.g. Sep 24, 2026. |
| project.access.request.pending.sent.today | Request pending · sent today at {time} | Aanvraag in behandeling · vandaag om {time} verzonden | Status of a request for access to a project sent today. {time} is a time of day, e.g. 10:42 AM. |
| project.access.request.title | Request access to this project | Toegang tot dit project aanvragen | Title of the page shown to a user who opens a project they are not a member of. |
| project.access.requested.description | Your request was sent to the project owners. They'll choose your project role. | Je aanvraag is naar de projecteigenaren verzonden. Zij kiezen je projectrol. | Shown right after the user asked the owners of a project for access to it. |
| project.access.requested.returning.description | You already requested access to this project. The project owners haven't responded yet. | Je hebt al toegang tot dit project aangevraagd. De projecteigenaren hebben nog niet gereageerd. | Shown when the user comes back to a project whose owners they already asked for access, within the hour. |
| project.access.requested.title | Access requested | Toegang aangevraagd | Title of the page shown once the user asked the owners of a project for access to it. |
| project.access.requested.your.message | Your message | Je bericht | Label of the message the user sent with their request for access to a project. |
| project.created.from.archive.copy | This project will be created using a copy of the content from this archive. | Dit project wordt gemaakt met een kopie van de inhoud van dit archief. |  |
| project.created.from.git.repository | This project will be created using the content from this GitHub repository. It starts as a copy, so you can customize it without affecting the original. | Dit project wordt gemaakt met de inhoud van deze GitHub-repository. Het begint als een kopie, zodat u het kunt aanpassen zonder het origineel te beïnvloeden. |  |
| project.creation.failed | Failed to create new project. | Nieuw project aanmaken mislukt. | Notification message displayed when the creation of a new project fails. |
| project.delete.failed | Failed to delete project | Project verwijderen mislukt | Presented when project delete fails. |
| project.description.save.success | Project description saved successfully | Projectbeschrijving succesvol opgeslagen | Notification message displayed when the project description were saved successfully |
| project.file.read.only | This file is read-only. To make changes, edit it in your personal workspace or start a review task. | Dit bestand is alleen-lezen. Om wijzigingen aan te brengen, bewerk het in uw persoonlijke werkruimte of start een beoordelingsopdracht. | Message shown when a project file is opened in read-only mode. |
| project.files.empty.state | To add files, edit the project in your workspace and then commit your changes. | Om bestanden toe te voegen, bewerk het project in uw werkruimte en commiteer vervolgens uw wijzigingen. | Message shown when the project has no files, informing users they can drag and drop files to add content. |
| project.name.save.success | Project name saved successfully | Projectnaam succesvol opgeslagen | Notification message displayed when the project name were saved successfully |
| project.not.available | Project not available | Project niet beschikbaar | Error message presented when the details of a project could not be loaded from the server. |
| project.not.linked.to.git | The current project is not linked to a Git repository. | Het huidige project is niet gekoppeld aan een Git-repository. | Message indicating that the current project is not linked to a Git repository. |
| project.read.only.dialog.title | Insufficient permissions | Onvoldoende rechten |  |
| project.read.only.warning | You do not have permission to write to the remote Git repository. This is most likely due to insufficient access rights. Please check your repository permissions or contact the repository maintainers for assistance. | U heeft geen toestemming om naar de externe Git-repository te schrijven. Dit komt waarschijnlijk door onvoldoende toegangsrechten. Controleer uw repositoryrechten of neem contact op met de repositorybeheerders voor hulp. |  |
| project.role.author.public.collaborator.unavailable.info | Authors already have full access to every public project, so the Collaborator role has no additional effect for them here | Auteurs hebben al volledige toegang tot elk openbaar project, dus de rol Medewerker heeft hier voor hen geen extra effect | Tooltip shown on the disabled Collaborator project-role option for an Author platform-role user on a public project. |
| project.role.collaborator.info | Can suggest changes to the project's files by creating review tasks from their suggestions | Kan wijzigingen aan de bestanden van het project voorstellen door beoordelingsopdrachten van hun suggesties te maken | Info tooltip describing what the project Collaborator role can do, shown regardless of disabled state. |
| project.role.contributor.locked.info | This user has the Contributor platform role, which only allows them to be a project Collaborator | Deze gebruiker heeft de platformrol Bijdrager, waardoor hij in projecten alleen Medewerker kan zijn | Tooltip shown on disabled Owner/Maintainer project-role options for a Contributor platform-role user. |
| project.role.maintainer.info | Can access and review the project's content | Heeft toegang tot de inhoud van het project en kan deze beoordelen | Info tooltip describing what the project Maintainer role can do, shown regardless of disabled state. |
| project.role.owner.info | Can manage project settings and members, and delete the project | Kan projectinstellingen en leden beheren en het project verwijderen | Info tooltip describing what the project Owner role can do, shown regardless of disabled state. |
| project.role.public.maintainer.unavailable.info | Public projects don't support the Maintainer role | Openbare projecten ondersteunen de rol Onderhouder niet | Tooltip shown on the disabled Maintainer project-role option when the project is public. |
| project.settings | Project settings | Projectinstellingen | Project settings page title |
| project.settings.account.section.title | Account credentials | Accountgegevens | The title of the account credentials section. |
| project.settings.delete.description | This action will have no impact on the data stored in the remote repository. | Deze actie heeft geen invloed op de gegevens die in de externe repository zijn opgeslagen. | Description of the delete action in project settings delete section |
| project.settings.delete.dialog.alert | This action is irreversible and all tasks created within the project will be permanently deleted. | Deze actie is onomkeerbaar en alle in het project aangemaakte taken worden definitief verwijderd. | The alert message in the delete confirmation dialog |
| project.settings.delete.dialog.confirmation | Are you sure you want to delete the project {project_name}? | Weet u zeker dat u project {project_name} wilt verwijderen? | Message in the delete confirmation dialog |
| project.settings.load.error | Failed to load project settings. | Laden van projectinstellingen mislukt. | Error message displayed when project settings loading failed |
| project.settings.save.label | Save settings | Instellingen opslaan | Label for the save button in project settings page |
| project.settings.save.success | Project settings saved successfully | Projectinstellingen succesvol opgeslagen | Notification message displayed when the project setting were saved successfully |
| project.settings.update.error | Failed to update project settings. | Bijwerken projectinstellingen mislukt. | Error message displayed when project settings could not be updated |
| project.type.dita.tooltip | DITA is for maps, topics, and related DITA files. With this type, you can organize publications, publish to PDF and WebHelp, review DITA quality in Problems, reuse content with keys and components, run translation jobs, search within the current map, and insert images and media from the Content library. | DITA is voor maps, topics en bijbehorende DITA-bestanden. Met dit type kun je publicaties organiseren, naar PDF en WebHelp publiceren, DITA-kwaliteit controleren in Problemen, content hergebruiken met keys en componenten, vertaaltaken uitvoeren, binnen de huidige map zoeken en afbeeldingen en media invoegen vanuit de Contentbibliotheek. | Shown on the DITA option when choosing project type, and on the type in settings. |
| project.type.generic | Generic | Generiek | Project type option for non-DITA content. DITA is the acronym and is not translated. |
| project.type.generic.tooltip | Generic is for projects that are not DITA. With this type, you can store any mix of files, including other XML formats such as DocBook or S1000D, Markdown, and non-XML documents. You can edit files, create review tasks, and search across the project. DITA-specific features are not available, including publications, PDF and WebHelp publishing, DITA quality checks in Problems, content reuse with keys and components, translation jobs, search within the current map, and inserting images and media from the Content library. | Generiek is voor projecten die geen DITA zijn. Met dit type kun je elke mix van bestanden opslaan, waaronder andere XML-formaten zoals DocBook of S1000D, Markdown en niet-XML-documenten. Je kunt bestanden bewerken, reviewtaken maken en het project doorzoeken. DITA-specifieke functies zijn niet beschikbaar, waaronder publicaties, PDF- en WebHelp-publicatie, DITA-kwaliteitscontroles in Problemen, hergebruik van content met keys en componenten, vertaaltaken, zoeken binnen de huidige map, en het invoegen van afbeeldingen en media vanuit de Contentbibliotheek. | Shown on the Generic option when choosing project type, and on the type in settings. |
| project.type.immutable.note | Project type can't be changed. | Kan later niet meer worden gewijzigd. | Shown under the project type selector and on settings. Type cannot be changed after creation. |
| project.url | Project URL | Project-URL | A project's URL input label. |
| project.visibility.private.tooltip | Private projects are accessible only for authors that are added as members on the project. | Privéprojecten zijn alleen toegankelijk voor auteurs die als leden aan het project zijn toegevoegd. |  |
| project.visibility.public.tooltip | Public projects are accessible to all organization authors. | Openbare projecten zijn toegankelijk voor alle organisatie-auteurs. |  |
| project.visibility.tooltip | Controls who can access this project. | Bepaalt wie toegang heeft tot dit project. |  |
| project_access_requested_add_member | To give them access, add them as a member of the project and choose their project role. | Voeg deze persoon toe als lid van het project en kies de projectrol om toegang te geven. | Tells the owners of a project how to give access to the user who asked for it. |
| project_access_requested_body | {0} requested access to the project: | {0} heeft toegang aangevraagd tot het project: | Body text for email sent to the owners of a project when a user asks for access to it. {0} is the name and email of the user asking for access. The project name follows the text. |
| project_access_requested_subject | [{0}] {1} requested access to the project "{2}" | [{0}] {1} heeft toegang aangevraagd tot het project "{2}" | Subject for email sent to the owners of a project when a user asks for access to it. {0} is the product name, {1} is the name of the user asking for access and {2} is the project name. |
| project_invalid_description | Project description must not exceed 2048 characters | Projectbeschrijving mag niet meer dan 2048 tekens bevatten | Error message shown when a project description is too long (over 2048 characters) |
| project_invalid_name | Project name is invalid | Projectnaam is ongeldig | Error message shown when a project name is invalid |
| project_member_added_body_collaborator | {0} added you as a collaborator on the project: | {0} heeft je als medewerker aan het project toegevoegd: | Body text for email sent when a user is added as a COLLABORATOR of a project. {0} is the name and email of the user who added them. |
| project_member_added_body_maintainer | {0} added you as a maintainer on the project: | {0} heeft je als onderhouder aan het project toegevoegd: | Body text for email sent when a user is added as a MAINTAINER of a project. {0} is the name and email of the user who added them. |
| project_member_added_body_owner | {0} added you as an owner on the project: | {0} heeft je als eigenaar aan het project toegevoegd: | Body text for email sent when a user is added as an OWNER of a project. {0} is the name and email of the user who added them. |
| project_member_added_subject | [{0}] {1} added you to the project "{2}" | [{0}] {1} heeft je toegevoegd aan het project "{2}" | Subject for email sent when a user is added as a member of a project. {0} is the product name, {1} is the name of the user who added them and {2} is the project name. |
| project_member_removed_body | {0} removed you from the project: | {0} heeft je uit het project verwijderd: | Body text for email sent when a user is removed from a project. {0} is the name and email of the user who removed them. |
| project_member_removed_subject | [{0}] You were removed from the project "{1}" | [{0}] Je bent verwijderd uit het project "{1}" | Subject for email sent when a user is removed from a project. {0} is the product name and {1} is the project name. |
| project_member_role_updated_body_to_collaborator | {0} changed your role to collaborator on the project: | {0} heeft je rol in het project gewijzigd naar medewerker: | Body text for email sent when a user's role was changed to COLLABORATOR in a project. {0} is the acting user's name and email. |
| project_member_role_updated_body_to_maintainer | {0} changed your role to maintainer on the project: | {0} heeft je rol in het project gewijzigd naar onderhouder: | Body text for email sent when a user's role was changed to MAINTAINER in a project. {0} is the acting user's name and email. |
| project_member_role_updated_body_to_owner | {0} changed your role to owner on the project: | {0} heeft je rol in het project gewijzigd naar eigenaar: | Body text for email sent when a user's role was changed to OWNER in a project. {0} is the acting user's name and email. |
| project_member_role_updated_subject | [{0}] Your role in the project "{1}" was updated | [{0}] Je rol in het project "{1}" is bijgewerkt | Subject for email sent when a user's role in a project is updated. {0} is the product name and {1} is the project name. |

| queue_p50_tooltip | The 50th percentile of time that builds spend in a queue, meaning 50% of the builds waited in the queue for this amount of time or less. | De 50e percentiel van de tijd die builds in een wachtrij doorbrengen, wat betekent dat 50% van de builds deze tijd of minder in de wachtrij heeft gewacht. | Tooltip explaining the meaning of queue "P50" metric |
| queued_average_tooltip | The average time spent for a build in the queue, calculated across all runs. | De gemiddelde tijd die een build in de wachtrij heeft doorgebracht, berekend over alle runs. | Tooltip explaining the meaning of "Average" metric for Queued column |
| queued_max_tooltip | The longest time spent for a build in the queue. | De langste tijd die een build in de wachtrij heeft doorgebracht. | Tooltip explaining the meaning of "Max" metric for Queued column |

| queued_p90_tooltip | The 90th percentile of time that builds spent in the queue, meaning 90% of the builds waited in the queue for this amount of time or less. | Het 90e percentiel van de tijd die builds in de wachtrij hebben doorgebracht, wat betekent dat 90% van de builds deze tijd of minder in de wachtrij hebben gewacht. | Tooltip explaining the meaning of "P90" metric for Queued column |

| read_more_tooltip | Open the User Guide for the {0} plugin. | Open de handleiding voor de plug-in {0}. | Tooltip for "Read more" link that opens the plugin documentation |
| read_only_because_guest_author | This editor is read-only because you are not assigned as a collaborator on the current task. | Deze editor is alleen-lezen omdat u niet als medewerker bent toegewezen aan de huidige taak. |  |
| read_only_because_in_submodule | This editor is read-only because the file is part of a submodule. Edit it in the project that supplies the submodule. | Deze editor is alleen-lezen omdat het bestand deel uitmaakt van een submodule. Bewerk het in het project dat de submodule levert. |  |
| read_only_because_not_in_task | This file is in read-only mode because it is referenced content. | Dit bestand bevindt zich in de modus alleen-lezen omdat het inhoud bevat waarnaar wordt verweisen. | Message shown when a user tries to edit a read-only file in web reviewer and the file is read-only because it is not part of the current editing task. |
| read_only_because_task_closed | The file is read-only because the task's owner has closed the review. | Het bestand is alleen-lezen omdat de eigenaar van de taak de review heeft gesloten. | Task is not active. Files in task are read-only and can be open read-only |
| read_only_because_task_integrated | This file is read-only because it belongs to a task that has been integrated | Dit bestand is alleen-lezen omdat het bij een taak hoort die is geïntegreerd | Task is not active. Files in task are read-only and can be open read-only |
| read_only_because_task_is_integrating | This file is read-only because it belongs to a task that is being integrated by an owner. | Dit bestand is alleen-lezen omdat het bij een taak hoort die door een eigenaar wordt geïntegreerd. | Task is being integrated by an owner. Files in task are read-only for the other users (both Reviewers and Owners). |
| read_only_files_could_not_be_updated | The following files could not be updated because they are read-only: | De volgende bestanden konden niet worden geüpdatet omdat zij alleen-lezen zijn: | Information message from the dialog that presents the list of read-only files that could not be updated |
| read_only_files_suggestion | You can make them editable and try again to update them with the merged content. | U kunt deze bewerkbaar maken en opnieuw proberen ze met de samengevoegde inhoud te updaten. | Suggestion to make read only files editable and then try again to integrate changes |
| real_time | Real time | Direct | One type of updates from server |
| real_time_notif_off | Failed to subscribe for real-time server updates.\nThe plugin will automatically retry subscribing.\nYou can report this problem if you see that the tasks are not updated correctly. | Abonnement voor directe serverupdates afsluiten mislukt.\nDe plug-in zal automatisch opnieuw proberen een abonnement af te sluiten.\nU kunt dit probleem rapporteren als u ziet dat de taken niet correct worden geüpdatet. | Details for the case when real time notifications are off |

| role_change_info_author | As an Author, you now have access to the Projects functionality, including the ability to create and manage projects. | Als Auteur heeft u nu toegang tot de projectfunctionaliteit, inclusief de mogelijkheid om projecten aan te maken en te beheren. | Info for users who become AUTHOR. |
| role_change_info_contributor | As a Contributor, you can be assigned as a collaborator on projects to suggest changes to their files by creating review tasks from your suggestions. | Als Bijdrager kunt u worden toegewezen als medewerker aan projecten om wijzigingen aan hun bestanden voor te stellen door beoordelingsopdrachten te maken vanuit uw suggesties. | Info for users who become CONTRIBUTOR, coming from a role other than AUTHOR. |
| role_change_info_contributor_from_author | As a Contributor, your access to the Projects functionality is now limited to that of a project collaborator: you can be assigned as a collaborator on projects to suggest changes to their files by creating review tasks from your suggestions. | Als Bijdrager is uw toegang tot de projectfunctionaliteit nu beperkt tot die van een projectmedewerker: u kunt worden toegewezen als medewerker aan projecten om wijzigingen aan hun bestanden voor te stellen door beoordelingsopdrachten te maken vanuit uw suggesties. | Info for users who become CONTRIBUTOR after previously being AUTHOR: unlike SME, they keep some project access, just limited to what a project collaborator has. |
| role_change_info_none | Your platform role has been set to "No license seat". | role_change_info_sme | As a Subject Matter Expert, you can create and manage review tasks. | Als Vakspecialist kunt u beoordelingsopdrachten maken en beheren. | Info for users who become SME, coming from a role other than AUTHOR. |
| role_change_info_sme_from_author | As a Subject Matter Expert, you no longer have access to the Projects functionality. | Als Vakspecialist heeft u geen toegang meer tot de projectfunctionaliteit. | Info for users who become SME after previously being AUTHOR. |
| role_change_info_sme_from_contributor | As a Subject Matter Expert, you still have access to review tasks, but no longer have access to the projects you were a collaborator on. | Als Vakspecialist heeft u nog steeds toegang tot beoordelingsopdrachten, maar geen toegang meer tot de projecten waarin u medewerker was. | Info for users who become SME after previously being CONTRIBUTOR: they keep working with review tasks, but lose the project-collaborator access they had (SME has no project access at all). |

| save_new_external_resources_message | The task contains modified documents that were originally stored on a remote server, as well as new documents.\n The modified documents are opened in separate editor tabs. You need to manually save them (according to the protocol required by the remote server).\n The newly added documents must be saved locally (click "Choose directory" to save them) and then manually uploaded on the remote server. | De taak bevat documenten die oorspronkelijk werden opgeslagen op een externe server, plus nieuwe documenten.\n De gewijzigde documenten worden geopend op aparte tabbladen van de editor. U moet deze documenten handmatig opslaan (conform het vereiste protocol op de externe server).\n De nieuw toegevoegde documenten moeten lokaal worden opgeslagen (klik op 'Map kiezen' om ze op te slaan) en vervolgens handmatig worden geüpload naar de externe server. | Message presented to the user when there are new external resources are applied. |
